# JAN-CSAA-001 Requirement Ledger

**Ledger ID:** `JAN-CSAA-001-LEDGER-001@0.1.0`

**Status:** Open Wave 1 authoring ledger; non-authoritative evidence record

**Purpose:** Preserve one row for every atomic `JAN-CSAA-001` requirement and every applicable inherited obligation while keeping authored text, independent verification, lifecycle performance, and later adoption separate.

**Authority rule:** Prepared under `JPWB-REG-005@1.0.0 REG-D-018`. This ledger creates no implementation, provider, topology, gate, oracle, or member authority.

## 2. Intake, atomicity, and reconciliation

An atomic 001 row contains one independently reviewable mandatory predicate. A comma-delimited completeness set remains one predicate only where it has one actor, one action, and one verification decision. Closed `JAN-CSAA-000` source rows retain their exact stable IDs and source clause grain. Named canon axioms or invariants remain one row unless their source presents independently actionable prohibitions.

| Source artifact | Exact version or identity | Affirmative mandatory rows | Prohibitive mandatory rows | Ledger rows | Extraction evidence |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `JAN-CSAA-001` catalog | `0.1.0 / Draft`, §16 | 177 | 54 | 231 | Mechanical row/modal reconciliation plus independently dispositionable actor/action/outcome review |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` | `0.3.0 / Normative / HYPOTHESIS` | 182 | 45 | 227 | Exact closed-ledger rows: REQ-040–205, REQ-642–685, REQ-736–750, REQ-754, REQ-783 |
| `JPWB-CON-000` | `1.3.0` | 6 | 8 | 14 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| `JPWB-DOC-001` | `1.1.0` | 3 | 7 | 10 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| `JPWB-DOC-002` | `1.2.0` | 3 | 4 | 7 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| `JPWB-DOC-003` | `1.3.0` | 7 | 9 | 16 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| `JPWB-DOC-004` | `1.3.0` | 12 | 6 | 18 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| `JPWB-REG-005` | `1.0.0` / REG-D-018 | 1 | 7 | 8 | Applicable concern-owned clauses decomposed in §4.2 |
| **Total** | — | **391** | **140** | **531** | 231 authored + 227 charter + 73 canon/register = 531 |

Reconciliation: **531 obligations = 531 unique ledger rows; difference = 0**. The inherited intake records the architecture, document-control, Wave 1 boundary, adversarial-review, and later-allocation surfaces identified during authoring. Independent completeness and applicability review of that inherited intake remains OPEN under `JAN-CSAA-001-LEDGER-GAP-001`; excluded charter ranges are concern-routed in §4.3 rather than silently treated as verified omissions.

## 3. JAN-CSAA-001-authored requirement rows

`PLANNED` means that the non-authoritative Draft records a candidate obligation and substantive authoring site; it does not claim implementation or verification. Every requirement row remains `NOT_RUN`, and neither state means that a provider, contract, test, implementation, later-owner artifact, or verification result exists.

| `CSAA-000-REQ-040` | `JAN-CSAA-000@0.3.0` / §4.2, L256 | SHALL — 'apps/rph-demo/tsconfig.json' extends the generated '.svelte-kit/tsconfig.json'. ### 4.3 Intake exclusions and concern routing

| Source surface not expanded into 001-owned rows | Rationale |
| --- | --- |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-001–039 | Charter identity, sponsor package, and whole-corpus capability declarations remain parent authority. 001's detailed allocation begins at REQ-180; architecture-relevant subject rules are carried from REQ-040. |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-206–641 | Detailed obligations belong to 002–011 or later whole-corpus phases. Main §17.2 records 001's exact boundary to each owner without importing its concern. |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-686–735, REQ-751–753, REQ-755–782 | Whole-corpus completion, implementation readiness/DoD, W0 mechanics, and other-member content are not 001 performance. Applicable no-existence/provider-neutral controls are carried as REQ-736–750, REQ-754, and REQ-783. |
| Canon professional-work object/lifecycle clauses not listed in §4.2 | 001 does not govern PWA, PWU, Undertaking, professional-objective, assurance-disposition, or Baseline semantics. Only architecture, authority, evidence, agent-boundary, persistence-safety, oracle, and drafting obligations engaged by 001 are ledgered. |

## 5. States, row rules, conflicts, and gaps

### 5.1 Allowed obligation dispositions

- `APPLICABLE_NOW`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_LATER_WAVE`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_LATER_LIFECYCLE`
- `DEFERRED_BY_CITED_AUTHORITY`
- `WAIVED_BY_CITED_AUTHORITY`
- `DIVERGENT_WITH_FINDING`
- `NOT_APPLICABLE_WITH_RATIONALE`

### 5.2 Allowed implementation states

- `NOT_STARTED`
- `PLANNED`
- `IMPLEMENTED`
- `NOT_REQUIRED_CURRENT_PHASE`

### 5.3 Allowed verification states

- `NOT_RUN`
- `PASSED`
- `FAILED`
- `BLOCKED`
- `INCONCLUSIVE`
- `STALE`
- `UNSUPPORTED`
- `NOT_REQUIRED_CURRENT_PHASE`

Implementation and verification are independent. No row is `PASSED`; every row remains `NOT_RUN`. A later allocation identifies an owner and gate but creates no artifact, authority, or performance.

### 5.4 Requirement-row rules

Each row retains stable ID, concern owner, exact source/version/section, non-weakened obligation, applicability, disposition authority, substantive site, implementation state, verification binding, oracle owner where applicable, and exact-subject evidence. Independent obligations are not compressed. Deferral, waiver, divergence, allocation, and not-applicability remain distinct from implementation and verification.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
